./feedback-logic.js:async function loadFeedback(businessId) {
./feedback-logic.js:    const tbody = document.getElementById('feedback-table-body');
./feedback-logic.js:    tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align:center; padding:20px;">Loading feedback...</td></tr>';
./feedback-logic.js:    try {
./feedback-logic.js:        const q = query(collection(db, "feedback"), where("businessId", "==", businessId), orderBy("createdAt", "desc"));
./feedback-logic.js:        const snapshot = await getDocs(q);
./feedback-logic.js:        
./feedback-logic.js:        if (snapshot.empty) {
./feedback-logic.js:            tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align:center; padding:20px;">No feedback received yet.</td></tr>';
./feedback-logic.js:            return;
./feedback-logic.js:        }
./feedback-logic.js:        tbody.innerHTML = snapshot.docs.map(doc => {
./feedback-logic.js:            const data = doc.data();
./feedback-logic.js:            const stars = '⭐'.repeat(data.rating || 0);
./feedback-logic.js:            const date = data.createdAt ? new Date(data.createdAt.seconds * 1000).toLocaleDateString() : 'N/A';
./feedback-logic.js:            
./feedback-logic.js:            let statusClass = 'badge-neutral';
./feedback-logic.js:            if (data.status === 'New') statusClass = 'badge-warning'; // Or success depending on preference
./feedback-logic.js:            if (data.status === 'Resolved') statusClass = 'badge-success';
./feedback-logic.js:            return `
./feedback-logic.js:                <tr>
./feedback-logic.js:                    <td style="font-weight:600;">${data.customerName || 'Anonymous'}</td>
./feedback-logic.js:                    <td style="color:#fbbf24; letter-spacing:2px;">${stars}</td>
./feedback-logic.js:                    <td style="color:#6b7280; max-width:300px; overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis; white-space:nowrap;">
./feedback-logic.js:                        ${data.message || ''}
./feedback-logic.js:                    </td>
./feedback-logic.js:                    <td>${date}</td>
./feedback-logic.js:                    <td><span class="badge ${statusClass}">${data.status || 'New'}</span></td>
./feedback-logic.js:                    <td><button class="btn" onclick="openReplyModal('${doc.id}', '${data.customerName}', '${data.message?.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}', ${data.rating})" style="padding:6px 12px; font-size:12px; border:1px solid #e5e7eb;">Reply</button></td>
./feedback-logic.js:                </tr>
./feedback-logic.js:            `;
./feedback-logic.js:        }).join('');
./feedback-logic.js:        
./feedback-logic.js:    } catch (err) {
./feedback-logic.js:        console.error(err);
./feedback-logic.js:        tbody.innerHTML = '<tr><td colspan="6" style="text-align:center; color:red;">Error loading data.</td></tr>';
./feedback-logic.js:    }
./feedback-logic.js:}

./funnel-settings.js:function setPatchValue(target, path, value) {
./funnel-settings.js:  if (value === undefined) return;
./funnel-settings.js:  const parts = path.split(".");
./funnel-settings.js:  const last = parts.pop();
./funnel-settings.js:  let ref = target;
./funnel-settings.js:  parts.forEach((part) => {
./funnel-settings.js:    if (!ref[part]) ref[part] = {};
./funnel-settings.js:    ref = ref[part];
./funnel-settings.js:  });
./funnel-settings.js:  ref[last] = value;
./funnel-settings.js:}
./funnel-settings.js:function collectPatch() {
./funnel-settings.js:  const funnel = currentCapabilities.reviewFunnel || {};
./funnel-settings.js:  const allowed = new Set(funnel.allowedPatchPaths || []);
./funnel-settings.js:  if (!allowed.size) return {};
./funnel-settings.js:  const patch = {};
./funnel-settings.js:  const allow = (path) => allowed.has(path);
./funnel-settings.js:  if (allow("happy.headline")) setPatchValue(patch, "happy.headline", ui.happyHeadline?.value?.trim() || "");
./funnel-settings.js:  if (allow("happy.ctaLabel")) setPatchValue(patch, "happy.ctaLabel", ui.happyCta?.value?.trim() || "");
./funnel-settings.js:  if (allow("happy.prompt")) setPatchValue(patch, "happy.prompt", ui.happyPrompt?.value?.trim() || "");
./funnel-settings.js:  if (allow("happy.googleReviewUrl"))
./funnel-settings.js:    setPatchValue(patch, "happy.googleReviewUrl", ui.googleUrl?.value?.trim() || "");
./funnel-settings.js:  if (allow("routing.thresholds.googleMin")) {
./funnel-settings.js:    setPatchValue(patch, "routing.enabled", true);
./funnel-settings.js:    setPatchValue(patch, "routing.type", "rating");
./funnel-settings.js:    setPatchValue(patch, "routing.thresholds.googleMin", Number(ui.ratingThreshold?.value || 4));
./funnel-settings.js:  }
./funnel-settings.js:  if (allow("unhappy.headline")) setPatchValue(patch, "unhappy.headline", ui.unhappyHeadline?.value?.trim() || "");
./funnel-settings.js:  if (allow("unhappy.message")) setPatchValue(patch, "unhappy.message", ui.unhappyMessage?.value?.trim() || "");
./funnel-settings.js:  if (allow("unhappy.followupEmail"))
./funnel-settings.js:    setPatchValue(patch, "unhappy.followupEmail", ui.followupOwner?.value?.trim() || "");
./funnel-settings.js:  if (allow("branding.primaryColor"))
./funnel-settings.js:    setPatchValue(patch, "branding.primaryColor", ui.primaryColor?.value || "#2563eb");
./funnel-settings.js:  if (allow("branding.logoUrl") && activeSettings.branding.logoUrl) {
./funnel-settings.js:    setPatchValue(patch, "branding.logoUrl", activeSettings.branding.logoUrl);
./funnel-settings.js:  }
./funnel-settings.js:  return patch;
./funnel-settings.js:}

./funnel-settings.js:      activeSettings.branding.logoUrl = logoUploadResult.url;
./funnel-settings.js:    }

./funnel-settings.js:    if (!token) {
./funnel-settings.js:      throw new Error("You need to be signed in to save your funnel.");
./funnel-settings.js:    }

./funnel-settings.js:      headers: {

./funnel-settings.js:        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,

./funnel-settings.js:      throw new Error(`${prefix}${message}`);
./funnel-settings.js:    }

./google-reviews.js:function initGoogleTokenClient() {

./google-reviews.js:        tokenClient = google.accounts.oauth2.initTokenClient({
./google-reviews.js:            client_id: '863497920392-f3nlicjmee2lomrrh9oihglc0a7tlsq2.apps.googleusercontent.com',
./google-reviews.js:            scope: 'https://www.googleapis.com/auth/business.manage',
./google-reviews.js:            callback: handleGoogleTokenResponse,

./google-reviews.js:async function fetchBusinessLocations(accessToken) {
./google-reviews.js:    try {
./google-reviews.js:        console.log("Step 1: Fetching Accounts...");
./google-reviews.js:        const accountsResp = await fetch('https://mybusinessaccountmanagement.googleapis.com/v1/accounts', {
./google-reviews.js:            headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${accessToken}` }
./google-reviews.js:        });
./google-reviews.js:        const accountsData = await accountsResp.json();
./google-reviews.js:        
./google-reviews.js:        if (!accountsData.accounts || accountsData.accounts.length === 0) {
./google-reviews.js:            throw new Error("No Google Business accounts found.");
./google-reviews.js:        }
./google-reviews.js:        let foundLocation = null;
./google-reviews.js:        // Loop through ALL accounts to find a location
./google-reviews.js:        for (const account of accountsData.accounts) {
./google-reviews.js:            console.log(`Checking account: ${account.name}`);
./google-reviews.js:            const locResp = await fetch(`https://mybusinessbusinessinformation.googleapis.com/v1/${account.name}/locations?readMask=name,title,metadata`, {
./google-reviews.js:                headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${accessToken}` }
./google-reviews.js:            });
./google-reviews.js:            const locData = await locResp.json();
./google-reviews.js:            if (locData.locations && locData.locations.length > 0) {
./google-reviews.js:                // Filter for verified or first available
./google-reviews.js:                foundLocation = locData.locations.find(l => l.metadata?.locationState?.isVerified) || locData.locations[0];
./google-reviews.js:                if (foundLocation) break; // Stop if we found one
./google-reviews.js:            }
./google-reviews.js:        }
./google-reviews.js:        if (foundLocation) {
./google-reviews.js:            console.log("SUCCESS! Found Location:", foundLocation);
./google-reviews.js:            
./google-reviews.js:            // Save to Firestore
./google-reviews.js:            const businessDocRef = doc(db, "businesses", currentUID);
./google-reviews.js:            await updateDoc(businessDocRef, {
./google-reviews.js:                businessName: foundLocation.title,
./google-reviews.js:                googleLocationId: foundLocation.name,
./google-reviews.js:                isGoogleConnected: true
./google-reviews.js:            });
./google-reviews.js:            // Update UI with REAL Business Name
./google-reviews.js:            document.getElementById('business-name').textContent = `Managing: ${foundLocation.title}`;
./google-reviews.js:            document.getElementById('user-email').textContent = "Business Profile Connected";
./google-reviews.js:            
./google-reviews.js:        } else {
./google-reviews.js:            console.warn("No locations found in any account.");
./google-reviews.js:            alert("Connected to Google, but no Business Profiles were found in this account.");
./google-reviews.js:            // Fallback to user info
./google-reviews.js:            const email = localStorage.getItem('google_user_email');
./google-reviews.js:            document.getElementById('business-name').textContent = "Connected (No Business Found)";
./google-reviews.js:            document.getElementById('user-email').textContent = email;
./google-reviews.js:        }
./google-reviews.js:    } catch (error) {
./google-reviews.js:        console.error("Error fetching business:", error);
./google-reviews.js:        document.getElementById('user-email').textContent = "Error: " + error.message;
./google-reviews.js:    }
./google-reviews.js:}
./google-reviews.js:function checkConnectionStatus() {
./google-reviews.js:    const accessToken = localStorage.getItem('google_access_token');
./google-reviews.js:    const userEmail = localStorage.getItem('google_user_email');
./google-reviews.js:    if (accessToken && userEmail) {
./google-reviews.js:        const userInfo = {
./google-reviews.js:            name: localStorage.getItem('google_user_name'),
./google-reviews.js:            email: userEmail,
./google-reviews.js:            picture: localStorage.getItem('google_user_avatar')
./google-reviews.js:        };
./google-reviews.js:        updateConnectionUI(true, userInfo);
./google-reviews.js:        // If connected, also try to fetch and display the linked business name
./google-reviews.js:        getDoc(doc(db, "businesses", currentUID)).then(docSnap => {
./google-reviews.js:            if (docSnap.exists() && docSnap.data().googleBusinessProfile) {
./google-reviews.js:                document.getElementById('business-name').textContent = `Managing: ${docSnap.data().googleBusinessProfile.businessName}`;
./google-reviews.js:            }

./google-reviews.js:function disconnectGoogle() {
./google-reviews.js:    const token = localStorage.getItem('google_access_token');
./google-reviews.js:    if (token) {
./google-reviews.js:        google.accounts.oauth2.revoke(token, () => {

./google-reviews.js:    localStorage.removeItem('google_user_email');

./google-reviews.js:    localStorage.removeItem('google_user_avatar');
./google-reviews.js:    if (currentUID) {

./google-reviews.js:        updateDoc(businessDocRef, {
./google-reviews.js:            isGoogleConnected: false,

./google-reviews.js:async function loadGoogleReviewsDashboard(uid) {
./google-reviews.js:    const contentArea = document.getElementById('google-reviews-content');
./google-reviews.js:    contentArea.innerHTML = renderLoadingSpinner();
./google-reviews.js:    try {
./google-reviews.js:        if (!db || !auth) throw new Error("Firebase not initialized");
./google-reviews.js:        const [interceptedResult, generatedResult, googleReviewsResult] = await Promise.all([
./google-reviews.js:            getDocs(query(collection(db, "feedback"), where("businessId", "==", uid), where("rating", "<=", 3))),
./google-reviews.js:            getDocs(query(collection(db, "feedback"), where("businessId", "==", uid), where("rating", ">=", 4))),
./google-reviews.js:            getDocs(query(collection(db, "businesses", uid, "google_reviews"), orderBy("review_creation_timestamp", "desc")))
./google-reviews.js:        ]);
./google-reviews.js:        const interceptedCount = interceptedResult.size;
./google-reviews.js:        const generatedCount = generatedResult.size;
./google-reviews.js:        
./google-reviews.js:        let googleReviews = [];
./google-reviews.js:        googleReviewsResult.forEach(doc => googleReviews.push(doc.data()));
./google-reviews.js:        const totalReviews = googleReviews.length;
./google-reviews.js:        const totalRating = googleReviews.reduce((acc, r) => acc + (r.rating || 0), 0);
./google-reviews.js:        const avgRating = totalReviews > 0 ? (totalRating / totalReviews).toFixed(1) : "N/A";
./google-reviews.js:        const stats = {
./google-reviews.js:            totalReviews,
./google-reviews.js:            generatedCount,
./google-reviews.js:            interceptedCount,
./google-reviews.js:            avgRating
./google-reviews.js:        };
./google-reviews.js:        
./google-reviews.js:        contentArea.innerHTML = `
./google-reviews.js:            ${renderRoiMetrics(stats)}
./google-reviews.js:            ${renderReviewsTable(googleReviews)}
./google-reviews.js:        `;
./google-reviews.js:    } catch (error) {
./google-reviews.js:        console.error("CRITICAL LOAD ERROR:", error);
./google-reviews.js:        const container = document.getElementById('google-reviews-content') || document.body;
./google-reviews.js:        if (error.message.includes("index") || error.code === "failed-precondition") {
./google-reviews.js:            container.innerHTML = `<div class="alert alert-warning m-4">...</div>`;
./google-reviews.js:        } else {
./google-reviews.js:            container.innerHTML = `<div class="alert alert-danger m-4">...</div>`;
./google-reviews.js:        }
./google-reviews.js:    }
./google-reviews.js:}

./demo.html:  <script>
./demo.html:    (function () {
./demo.html:      const portal = document.getElementById("portal");
./demo.html:      const ratingButtons = document.querySelectorAll(".rating-button");
./demo.html:      const currentRatingValue = document.getElementById("currentRatingValue");
./demo.html:      const changeRatingLink = document.getElementById("changeRatingLink");
./demo.html:      const ratingField = document.getElementById("ratingField");
./demo.html:      const lowPanel = document.getElementById("lowPanel");
./demo.html:      const highPanel = document.getElementById("highPanel");
./demo.html:      const feedbackForm = document.getElementById("feedbackForm");
./demo.html:      let currentRating = 0;
./demo.html:      function applyRatingState() {
./demo.html:        // update UI state classes
./demo.html:        portal.classList.add("has-rating");
./demo.html:        if (currentRating <= 3) {
./demo.html:          portal.classList.add("low-rating");
./demo.html:          portal.classList.remove("high-rating");
./demo.html:        } else {
./demo.html:          portal.classList.add("high-rating");
./demo.html:          portal.classList.remove("low-rating");
./demo.html:        }
./demo.html:        currentRatingValue.textContent = currentRating + "★";
./demo.html:        ratingField.value = String(currentRating);
./demo.html:        ratingButtons.forEach((btn) => {
./demo.html:          const value = Number(btn.dataset.rating);
./demo.html:          btn.classList.toggle("selected", value === currentRating);
./demo.html:        });
./demo.html:      }
./demo.html:      ratingButtons.forEach((btn) => {
./demo.html:        btn.addEventListener("click", () => {
./demo.html:          const value = Number(btn.dataset.rating);
./demo.html:          if (!value) return;
./demo.html:          currentRating = value;
./demo.html:          applyRatingState();
./demo.html:        });
./demo.html:      });
./demo.html:      changeRatingLink.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
./demo.html:        event.preventDefault();
./demo.html:        currentRating = 0;
./demo.html:        portal.classList.remove("has-rating", "low-rating", "high-rating", "feedback-sent");
./demo.html:        currentRatingValue.textContent = "–";
./demo.html:        ratingField.value = "";
./demo.html:        ratingButtons.forEach((btn) => btn.classList.remove("selected"));
./demo.html:      });
./demo.html:      if (feedbackForm) {
./demo.html:        feedbackForm.addEventListener("submit", async (event) => {
./demo.html:          event.preventDefault();
./demo.html:          if (!currentRating) {
./demo.html:            alert("Please select a rating first.");
./demo.html:            return;
./demo.html:          }
./demo.html:          const formData = new FormData(feedbackForm);
./demo.html:          formData.set("rating", String(currentRating));
./demo.html:          try {
./demo.html:            await fetch(feedbackForm.action, {
./demo.html:              method: feedbackForm.method,
./demo.html:              body: formData,
./demo.html:              headers: {
./demo.html:                Accept: "application/json",
./demo.html:              },
./demo.html:            });
./demo.html:          } catch (err) {
./demo.html:            // גם אם יש בעיית רשת – עדיין נציג תודה בצד הלקוח
./demo.html:            console.error("Formspree error", err);
./demo.html:          }
./demo.html:          portal.classList.add("feedback-sent");
./demo.html:        });
./demo.html:      }
./demo.html:    })();
./demo.html:  </script>
./demo.html:  <script type="module">
./demo.html:    import { db, doc, updateDoc, arrayUnion } from "./firebase-config.js";
./demo.html: 
./demo.html:    const sendBtn = document.getElementById("sendfeedback");
./demo.html:    const feedbackForm = document.getElementById("feedbackForm");
./demo.html: 
./demo.html:    if (sendBtn && feedbackForm) {
./demo.html:      sendBtn.addEventListener("click", async (e) => {
./demo.html:        e.preventDefault();
./demo.html: 
./demo.html:        const name = document.getElementById("name")?.value || "Anonymous";
./demo.html:        const comment = document.getElementById("feedback")?.value || "";
./demo.html:        const rating = window.currentRating || 1;
./demo.html: 
./demo.html:        console.log("🟡 DEBUG MODE: Review submission started");
./demo.html: 
./demo.html:        const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);

./session-data.js:        fetchDoc("businessProfiles", user.uid),
./session-data.js:        fetchDoc("businesses", user.uid),
./session-data.js:        fetchDoc("subscriptions", user.uid)
./session-data.js:    ]);
